For superincreasing gaps, length ~K^2, then N~K², objective J=2K² M_p/K²=J_p no improvement. For linear spacing, pair offsets collide (i+j same), coefficients add by number of pairs ~O(K), max ~M_p/K, objective J_p no improvement. Example blocks equally spaced: cross lag offset t G has (K-|t|) pairs, coefficient sum ~ (K-|t|) M_p/K²; max near t=0 ~K M_p/K²=M_p/K. N~K W, J=2K W M_p/K=J_p. So no improvement. To reduce, need distribute offsets so max adds less; that's equivalent to designing sequence.

Maybe we can use multiple low-J factors with random offsets to reduce peak average. But complex.

`optimize_direct_length` details:
```
def optimize_direct_length(n, budget, seed, parent=None):
    t0=time.monotonic(); deadline=t0+budget
    best_a=None; best_sc=inf
    # candidate list from inits
    rng=...
    inits=generate_inits(n,rng,parent,cap=max(8, ...))
    for arr in inits[:cap]: sc=exact; update
    # run L-BFGS on top inits while time
    candidates = sorted by score (we can compute all scores)
    # maybe also DE if n<=128 and budget>20
    if n <= 128 and time > 30:
        maxfev=...
        try de_res=...
        if de_res.success? use x
        polish
    # Then iterative perturb/quick for top 5 starts until time
    for start in top starts:
        while time left:
            p=perturb_best(start,rng)
            sc,seq=quick_optimize(p, deadline)
            update
    return best
```

```
def gen_compositions(rem_n, rem_sum, cur, out):
    if rem_n == 1:
        out.append(cur + [rem_sum]); return
    for v in range(rem_sum + 1):
        cur.append(v)
        gen_compositions(rem_n - 1, rem_sum - v, cur, out)
        cur.pop()
```

```
def soft_active_weights(d, gamma, top_frac):
    d = d - dmax
    # candidate indices where d > dmax - tol (e.g., top 10% or all if gamma finite)
    weights = np.exp(gamma*d)
    set small to0
    normalize
```

`subgrad_step_soft(a, eta, gamma, top_frac_or_k)`:
- d, S = d_from_a(a); dmax; if dmax<=0 return.

- If use_all: p=np.exp(gamma*(d-dmax)); p[p<1e-12]=0; normalize.
- h=corr_pair(a,p); hmax=max; if small return.

```
Construct meta affine:
```
def construct_meta_affine(x, n, r):
    t=np.linspace(0,1,n)
    val=np.ones(n)
    # first param base shift? x[0]
    val = 1.0 + 0.3*x[0] maybe
    for m in range(r):
        amp = 0.2 + 0.5*abs(x[1+2*m])? use x directly
        shift = x[2+2*m]
        f = m+1
        val += x[1+2*m] * np.cos(2π f t + shift)
    val=np.clip(val,0,None)
    if sum<1e-6: val=np.ones(n)
    return normalize_a(val)
```
Params are cos amplitude and phase? If phase in cos, amplitude can be negative; better use amplitude and phase. But DE explores. Use `val += amp * (cos(2π f t)*cos(phase) - sin? )` Actually a amplitude+phase pair. To ensure smooth, frequencies up to r. Bounds amp [-2,2], phase [-π,π]. val base 1 + maybe xperturb. If val negative, clip. This family includes asymmetric via sin.

Log meta:
```
def construct_meta_log(x,n,r):
   t=...
   logv=np.zeros(n)
   # x[0] global bias maybe 0
   for m in range(r):
       amp = x[1+2*m]
       ph = x[2+2*m]
       f=m+1
       logv += amp * np.cos(2π f t + ph)
   logv = np.clip(logv, -30, 30)
   a=np.exp(logv - np.max(logv))
   if sum<...: return ones
   return normalize_a(a)
```
Log can create sharply varying positive shape. R maybe 6 (13 params), frequencies 1..6. Bounds amp [-8,8]? Could make zeros but normalized positive exp; if amp too large, shape multi-modal. DE range [-8,8] for amp, phase [-π,π]. Use base 0. Sum normalized.
```

```
Construct affine:
```
def meta_affine(x,n,r):
    t=np.linspace(0,1,n)
    val=np.ones(n)
    if x.size >=1: val += 0.2*x[0]
    idx=1
    for f in range(1,r+1):
       if idx+1>=len(x): break
       amp=x[idx]; ph=x[idx+1]
       c=np.cos(2*math.pi*f*t + ph)
       val += amp*c
       idx+=2
    val=np.nan_to_num(val, nan=0, posinf=1e6, neginf=0)
    val=np.clip(val,0,None)
    if val.sum()<1e-6: val=np.ones(n)
    return normalize_a(val)
```
Bounds: if r params +1. amplitude [-3,3], phase [-π,π], base [-2,2]. But val can zero; normalize.
Log:
```
def meta_log(x,n,r):
    t=...
    logv=np.zeros(n)
    for f in 1..r:
       amp,ph
       logv += amp * np.cos(2π f t + ph)
    logv=np.nan_to_num
    logv=np.clip(logv,-30,30)
    a=np.exp(logv - np.max(logv))
    if sum<1e-12: a=ones
    return normalize_a(a)
```
Bounds amp [-8,8], phase.
Gauss:
```
def meta_gauss(x,n,r):
    t=...
    val=1.0 + 0.2*x[0] if size>0
    idx=1
    for g in range(r):
       center=0.5+0.5*np.tanh(x[idx])  # in [0,1]
       width=0.01+0.2*np.exp(0.5*x[idx+1])? or exp(-?)
       amp=x[idx+2]
       val += amp * np.exp(-((t-center)/width)**2)
       idx+=3
    clip, normalize
```
Bounds center [-5,5] tanh, width [-3,1] exp, amp [-3,3]. Could produce negative shapes.
```

```
Implementation:
```
def subgrad_polish_soft(a, max_iters, deadline, configs=None):
  a=normalize; best...
  if configs is None: configs=[(50,1.0,None),(200,0.2,None),(1000,0.05,None),(50,0,5),(100,0,3)]
  etas=(0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2)
  for it in range(max_iters):
    if time>deadline: break
    d,S=d_from_a(a)
    if d.size==0: break
    dmax=float(d.max())
    if not finite: break
    iter_best_sc=best_sc; iter_best_a=a; cur_sc=best_sc; cur_a=a
    for gamma,tf,tc in configs:
       if time>deadline: break
       w=soft_active_weights(d,gamma,tf,tc)
       h=corr_pair(a,w)
       hmax=float(h.max()) if h.size else 0
       if hmax<=1e-14: continue
       for eta in etas:
          q=a*np.exp(-eta*h/hmax)
          q=normalize(q)
          sc=exact_score(q)
          if finite and sc<best_sc: ...
          if finite and sc<cur_sc: cur...
          if time>deadline: break
    if cur_sc < iter_best_sc:
       a=cur_a
    else:
       break
  return best_sc,best_a
```
Need if `soft_active_weights` with top_count=0 means no top? Use top_fraction=1 for all. For top_count fixed, top_fraction=0.

`soft_active_weights`:
```
def soft_active_weights(d, gamma, top_fraction=1.0, top_count=None):
   m=len(d); dmax=float(d.max()); w=np.zeros(m)
   if dmax<=0: return w
   if top_count is not None and top_count>0:
      k=min(m,max(1,int(top_count)))
      if k<m:
        idx=np.argpartition(d, m-k)[m-k:]
        w[idx]=1.0
        mask=w>0
      else: mask all
   elif top_fraction is not None and top_fraction<1:
      k=max(1,int(m*top_fraction)); ...
   else: mask all
   z=gamma*(d[mask]-dmax)
   # clip z below -50 to avoid underflow? exp(-50) 1e-22; underflow to0 okay
   vals=np.exp(z)
   valid=vals>0
   if not any: w[mask]=1; sum=...
   else: w[mask]=vals; sum=w.sum(); return w/sum

Potential bug in `soft_score_grad`: `pen = 2.0 * PEN_SUM * (S - 1.0)` but score penalty should be `PEN_SUM*(S-1)^2`; derivative `2*PEN_SUM*(S-1)`. Correct.

But objective score in LSE should be `2*L` not `2*n*max/S²`? d = n*c/S²; exact max Cmax = S²/n * max d? exact =2n cmax/S² =2 max d. So 2L approximates exact. Good.

Now `corr_pair` returns h_i=∑ p_{i+j} a_j. For gradient of LSE with w over d, derivative of d_j = n c_j/S². ∂d_j/∂a_i = n/S² * 2a_{j-i} - 2n c_j/(S^3). Sum j w_j: =2n/S² ∑w_j a_{j-i} - 2n/S^3 ∑ w_j c_j? Wait c_j not d_j. Since d_j=n c_j/S², ∑w_j c_j = S²/n LDL? Hmm previous grad `- (4*LDS)/S` maybe approximates? Let's derive to ensure no sign.
d_j = n c_j/S².
∂c_j/∂a_i = 2a_{j-i}.
∂d_j = (n/S²)2a_{j-i} - (2n c_j/S³).

Let's produce final code.
</think>

```python
"""
Search for a non-negative step-function sequence minimizing
    2 * n * max(conv(a, a)) / sum(a)^2.

This version is targeted at large, dense sequences (ideally the 10240-length
parent if provided).  It combines:
  - exact FFT self-convolution scoring,
  - LSE-smoothed L-BFGS-B continuation,
  - soft-active-set multiplicative subgradient polish,
  - global low-dimensional trigonometric meta-optimization,
  - random/perturbative restarts.

The final candidate is normalized to sum 1 and returned as a plain Python list.
"""

import time
import math
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize, differential_evolution

PEN_SUM = 1e-3

# Calibrated evaluation timings
CALIB_N = 0
CALIB_T = 0.003

# Globals for meta-objective used by differential_evolution
META_N = 0
META_R = 0
META_MODE = 0


def next_power_of_two(m):
    m = int(max(1, m))
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def normalize_a(a, target=1.0):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, None)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s < 1e-14:
        return np.full(a.size, float(target) / float(a.size), dtype=np.float64)
    return (a * (float(target) / s)).astype(np.float64, copy=False)


def resample_sequence(a, n):
    a = np.nan_to_num(np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel(), nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    m = int(a.size)
    n = int(n)
    if m == 0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(max(1, n))
    if m == n:
        return a.copy()
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    return np.interp(x_new, x_old, a).astype(np.float64)


def fast_self_conv(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    m = 2 * n - 1
    N = next_power_of_two(m)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    out = np.fft.irfft(A * A, N)[:m]
    return np.nan_to_num(out, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)


def corr_pair(a, w):
    """
    Returns h_i = sum_j w_{i+j} a_j for i=0..n-1,
    where a has length n and w has length m.
    This is the bilinear sensitivity matrix for self-convolution coefficients.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(a.size)
    m = int(w.size)
    if n == 0 or m == 0:
        return np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    M = n + m - 1
    N = next_power_of_two(M)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a[::-1], N)
    W = np.fft.rfft(w, N)
    full = np.fft.irfft(A * W, N)[:M]
    return full[n - 1:2 * n - 1]


def d_from_a(a):
    a = normalize_a(a)
    n = len(a)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s < 1e-14 or n == 0:
        return np.zeros(2 * n - 1, dtype=np.float64), max(s, 1e-14)
    c = fast_self_conv(a)
    d = (float(n) * c) / (s * s)
    d = np.maximum(d, 0.0)
    return d, s


def exact_score_a(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf")
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s < 1e-12:
        return float("inf")
    c = fast_self_conv(a)
    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(cmax) or cmax < 0.0:
        cmax = 0.0
    return float(2.0 * n * cmax / (s * s))


def lse_score_grad(a, beta):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(a.size)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if n == 0 or s < 1e-8:
        return 1e12, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    beta = float(beta)
    if not np.isfinite(beta) or beta <= 1e-6:
        beta = 100.0

    d, s = d_from_a(a)
    dmax = float(np.max(d)) if d.size else 0.0
    if not np.isfinite(dmax) or dmax <= 0.0:
        return 1e12, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    z = beta * (d - dmax)
    z = np.nan_to_num(z, nan=-700.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=-700.0)
    z = np.clip(z, -700.0, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(z)
    sum_e = float(e.sum())
    if sum_e <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(sum_e):
        return 1e12, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    w = e / sum_e
    L = dmax + math.log(sum_e) / beta
    lds = float(np.dot(w, d))
    h = corr_pair(a, w)

    score = 2.0 * L + PEN_SUM * (s - 1.0) * (s - 1.0)
    grad = (4.0 * float(n) * h) / (s * s)
    grad -= (4.0 * lds) / s
    grad += 2.0 * PEN_SUM * (s - 1.0)

    grad = np.nan_to_num(grad, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    grad = np.clip(grad, -1e8, 1e8)
    return float(score), grad


def fun_lse(a, beta):
    return lse_score_grad(a, float(beta))


def calibrate_exact_time(a):
    global CALIB_N, CALIB_T
    n = int(np.asarray(a).size)
    if n < 128:
        CALIB_N = max(1, n)
        CALIB_T = 2e-5
        return

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    reps = 8
    for _ in range(reps):
        exact_score_a(a)
    dt = (time.monotonic() - t0) / float(max(1, reps))
    CALIB_N = n
    CALIB_T = max(3e-5, min(0.1, dt))
    print(f"Calibrated exact eval time for n={n}: {dt:.6f}s")


def estimate_exact_time(n):
    n = int(max(1, n))
    if CALIB_N > 0 and CALIB_T > 0:
        if n == CALIB_N:
            return CALIB_T
        return CALIB_T * (float(n) * math.log(n + 2.0)) / (float(CALIB_N) * math.log(CALIB_N + 2.0))
    return 3e-5 * math.log2(max(2.0, float(n))) + 1.0e-3 * max(0.0, float(n) / 8192.0)


def estimate_lse_time(n):
    return estimate_exact_time(n) * 2.6 + 2e-4


def estimate_meta_time(n):
    return estimate_exact_time(n) * 1.2 + 4e-3


def estimate_maxfun(t_left, n):
    t_left = float(max(0.0, t_left))
    if t_left < 6.0:
        return 0
    if n < 128:
        est = 8e-5
    else:
        est = estimate_lse_time(n)
    mf = int((0.75 * t_left) / max(est, 1e-5))
    return max(0, min(3500, mf))


def run_lbfgs(a0, beta, deadline=None, maxfun=None):
    a = normalize_a(a0)
    n = len(a)
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf"), a

    if deadline is not None:
        rem = float(deadline - time.monotonic())
        if rem < 8.0:
            return exact_score_a(a), a
        cap = estimate_maxfun(rem, n)
        if cap < 50:
            return exact_score_a(a), a
        if maxfun is None:
            maxfun = cap
        else:
            maxfun = int(min(int(maxfun), cap))
    else:
        if maxfun is None:
            maxfun = 1200

    maxfun = int(max(50, maxfun))
    maxiter = max(30, maxfun)

    bounds = [(0.0, 20.0)] * n
    opts = {
        "ftol": 1e-14,
        "gtol": 1e-11,
        "maxiter": int(maxiter),
        "maxfun": int(maxfun),
        "maxls": 50,
    }

    try:
        res = minimize(
            fun_lse,
            a,
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            jac=True,
            args=(float(beta),),
            bounds=bounds,
            options=opts,
        )
    except Exception:
        return exact_score_a(a), a

    if getattr(res, "x", None) is not None:
        a_out = normalize_a(res.x)
    else:
        a_out = a

    sc = exact_score_a(a_out)
    if not np.isfinite(sc) or sc > 1e8:
        a_out = a
        sc = exact_score_a(a)
    return float(sc), a_out


def soft_active_weights(d, gamma, top_fraction=1.0, top_count=None):
    d = np.asarray(d, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    m = int(d.size)
    w = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
    if m == 0:
        return w

    dmax = float(np.max(d))
    if not np.isfinite(dmax) or dmax <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64) / float(m)

    use_all = True
    if top_count is not None and top_count > 0:
        k = int(min(m, max(1, top_count)))
        if k < m:
            idx = np.argpartition(d, m - k)[m - k:]
            mask = np.zeros(m, dtype=bool)
            mask[idx] = True
            use_all = False
        else:
            mask = np.ones(m, dtype=bool)
    elif top_fraction is not None and top_fraction < 1.0:
        k = int(max(1, min(m, int(m * float(top_fraction)))))
        idx = np.argpartition(d, m - k)[m - k:]
        mask = np.zeros(m, dtype=bool)
        mask[idx] = True
        use_all = False
    else:
        mask = None

    if mask is None:
        z = gamma * (d - dmax)
        z = np.clip(z, -700.0, 0.0)
        vals = np.exp(z)
        vals = np.nan_to_num(vals, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
        s = float(vals.sum())
        if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
            return np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64) / float(m)
        return vals / s

    z = gamma * (d[mask] - dmax)
    z = np.clip(z, -700.0, 0.0)
    vals = np.exp(z)
    vals = np.nan_to_num(vals, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    w[mask] = vals
    s = float(w.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        w = zeros = np.zeros(m)
        w[mask] = 1.0
        s = float(w.sum())
        if s <= 0.0:
            return np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64) / float(m)
    return w / s


def subgrad_step_soft(a, w, eta):
    n = len(a)
    if n == 0:
        return a
    h = corr_pair(a, w)
    h = np.nan_to_num(h, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    hmax = float(np.max(np.abs(h))) if h.size else 0.0
    if hmax <= 1e-14:
        return a
    q = a * np.exp(-float(eta) * h / hmax)
    return normalize_a(q)


def subgrad_polish_soft(a, max_iters, deadline, configs=None):
    a = normalize_a(a)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = exact_score_a(a)
    if not configs:
        configs = [
            (50.0, 1.0, None),
            (200.0, 0.25, None),
            (1000.0, 0.08, None),
            (50.0, 0, 8),
            (100.0, 0, 3),
        ]
    etas = (0.015, 0.035, 0.075, 0.15, 0.30)

    for _ in range(int(max_iters)):
        if deadline is not None and time.monotonic() > float(deadline):
            break

        d, _ = d_from_a(a)
        if d.size == 0:
            break
        dmax = float(np.max(d))
        if not np.isfinite(dmax) or dmax <= 0.0:
            break

        iter_best_sc = best_sc
        iter_best_a = a
        cur_sc = best_sc
        cur_a = a

        for gamma, frac, topc in configs:
            if deadline is not None and time.monotonic() > float(deadline):
                break
            w = soft_active_weights(d, gamma, frac, topc)
            if w.sum() <= 0.0:
                continue
            for eta in etas:
                if deadline is not None and time.monotonic() > float(deadline) and abs(eta) < 0.15:
                    break
                q = subgrad_step_soft(a, w, eta)
                sc = exact_score_a(q)
                if np.isfinite(sc):
                    if sc < best_sc:
                        best_sc = float(sc)
                        best_a = q.copy()
                    if sc < cur_sc:
                        cur_sc = float(sc)
                        cur_a = q.copy()

        if cur_sc < iter_best_sc - 1e-12:
            a = cur_a
        else:
            break

    return float(best_sc), best_a


def add_candidate(inits, cap, arr):
    if inits is None or cap <= 0:
        return
    if len(inits) >= int(cap):
        return
    a = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=0.0)
    mn = float(np.min(a))
    if mn < 0.0:
        a = a - mn + 1e-4
    p = normalize_a(a)
    inits.append(p)


def generate_inits(n, rng, parent, cap=12):
    n = int(n)
    inits = []
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)

    if parent is not None and parent.size > 0:
        pr = resample_sequence(parent, n)
        add_candidate(inits, cap, pr)
        add_candidate(inits, cap, pr[::-1])

    add_candidate(inits, cap, np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))

    for alpha in (0.25, 0.5, 0.9, 1.5, 2.5):
        add_candidate(inits, cap, 1.0 + alpha * np.abs(2.0 * x - 1.0))

    for f in (1, 2, 3, 4):
        add_candidate(inits, cap, 1.0 + 0.5 * np.cos(2.0 * math.pi * f * x))
        add_candidate(inits, cap, 1.0 + 0.5 * np.sin(2.0 * math.pi * f * x))

    for alpha in (0.4, 0.8, 1.4):
        add_candidate(inits, cap, 1.0 + alpha * (2.0 * x - 1.0))
        add_candidate(inits, cap, 1.0 - alpha * (2.0 * x - 1.0))

    for rate in (0.8, 1.8, 3.2):
        add_candidate(inits, cap, np.exp(-rate * x))
        add_candidate(inits, cap, np.exp(-rate * (1.0 - x)))
        add_candidate(inits, cap, np.exp(-rate * x) + np.exp(-rate * (1.0 - x)))

    for width in (0.08, 0.14, 0.22):
        for center in (0.25, 0.70):
            add_candidate(
                inits,
                cap,
                np.exp(-(((x - center) / width) ** 2))
                + np.exp(-(((x + 0.5 - center) / width) ** 2)),
            )

    Nf = next_power_of_two(n)
    for _ in range(5):
        freqs = np.fft.rfftfreq(Nf) * Nf
        power = 1.0 / ((np.abs(freqs) + 1.0) ** float(rng.uniform(1.6, 4.0)))
        power[0] = 0.0
        phase = rng.uniform(-math.pi, math.pi, size=len(power))
        spec = power * (np.cos(phase) + 1j * np.sin(phase))
        spec[0] = 0.0
        noise = np.fft.irfft(spec, Nf)[:n]
        noise = noise - float(noise.mean())
        add_candidate(inits, cap, 1.0 + float(rng.uniform(0.4, 1.2)) * noise)

    for _ in range(4):
        vals = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        K = int(rng.integers(2, 6))
        maxf = max(2, n // 6)
        for _ in range(K):
            f = int(rng.integers(1, maxf + 1))
            amp = float(rng.uniform(0.08, 0.6))
            ph = float(rng.uniform(-math.pi, math.pi))
            vals += amp * np.cos(2.0 * math.pi * f * x + ph)
        add_candidate(inits, cap, vals)

    for nb in (8, 16, 32):
        if nb > n:
            nb = max(2, n // 2)
        block_vals = 0.1 + 0.9 * rng.random(nb)
        per = max(1, n // nb)
        arr = np.repeat(block_vals, per)
        if arr.size < n:
            arr = np.concatenate([arr, np.repeat(block_vals[-1], n - arr.size)])
        add_candidate(inits, cap, arr[:n])

    for _ in range(3):
        add_candidate(inits, cap, 0.1 + 0.9 * rng.random(n))

    if len(inits) > cap:
        inits = inits[:cap]
    return inits


def perturb_best(a, rng, strength=None):
    p = normalize_a(a)
    n = len(p)
    if n == 0:
        return p
    if strength is None:
        strength = float(rng.uniform(0.12, 0.65))
    mode = int(rng.integers(0, 9))

    if mode == 0:
        q = p * np.exp(strength * rng.standard_normal(n))
    elif mode == 1:
        t = float(rng.uniform(0.08, 0.35))
        q = (1.0 - t) * p + t * (np.ones(n) / float(n))
    elif mode == 2:
        q = p[::-1].copy()
        q = q * np.exp(0.12 * rng.standard_normal(n))
    elif mode == 3:
        d, _ = d_from_a(p)
        if d.size:
            w = soft_active_weights(d, float(rng.uniform(80.0, 700.0)), 0.08, None)
            q = subgrad_step_soft(p, w, strength * float(rng.uniform(0.4, 1.2)))
        else:
            q = p.copy()
    elif mode == 4:
        logp = np.log(p + 1e-300)
        noise = rng.standard_normal(n)
        k = min(31, max(3, int(n) // 250))
        if k % 2 == 0:
            k += 1
        if k >= 3 and k <= n:
            c = k // 2
            sig = max(1.0, c / 2.0)
            kern = np.exp(-((np.arange(k) - c) ** 2) / (2.0 * sig * sig))
            kern = kern / float(kern.sum())
            noise = np.convolve(noise, kern, mode="same")
        noise = noise - float(noise.mean())
        logp = logp + strength * 0.45 * noise
        q = np.exp(logp - float(np.max(logp)))
    elif mode == 5:
        x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
        vshape = 1.0 + strength * np.abs(2.0 * x - 1.0)
        q = p * vshape
    elif mode == 6:
        k = int(rng.integers(5, max(7, n // 120)))
        if k % 2 == 0:
            k += 1
        k = min(k, n if n % 2 == 1 else n - 1)
        if k < 3:
            k = 3
        c = k // 2
        sig = max(1.0, c / 2.0)
        kern = np.exp(-((np.arange(k) - c) ** 2) / (2.0 * sig * sig))
        kern = kern / float(kern.sum())
        q = np.convolve(p, kern, mode="same")
    elif mode == 7:
        f = int(rng.integers(2, max(3, n // 50) + 1))
        ph = float(rng.uniform(-math.pi, math.pi))
        x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
        q = p * (1.0 + 0.25 * strength * np.cos(2.0 * math.pi * f * x + ph))
    else:
        q = p * np.exp((strength * 0.7) * (2.0 * rng.random(n) - 1.0))

    q = np.clip(q, 0.0, None)
    return normalize_a(q)


def quick_optimize(a, deadline):
    a = normalize_a(a)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = exact_score_a(a)

    for beta in (120.0, 400.0, 1500.0, 6000.0, 20000.0, 70000.0):
        if deadline is not None and time.monotonic() > float(deadline) - 12.0:
            break
        rem = max(0.0, float(deadline) - time.monotonic())
        mf = estimate_maxfun(rem, len(a))
        if mf < 50:
            break
        sc, a2 = run_lbfgs(a, beta, deadline, mf)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = float(sc)
            best_a = a2.copy()
        a = normalize_a(a2)

    if deadline is not None and time.monotonic() < float(deadline) - 20.0:
        rem = max(0.0, float(deadline) - time.monotonic())
        mf = estimate_maxfun(rem, len(a))
        if mf >= 50:
            sc, a2 = run_lbfgs(best_a, 250000.0, deadline, mf)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = float(sc)
                best_a = a2.copy()

    return float(best_sc), best_a


def optimize_trajectory(a_start, deadline, rng, label):
    a = normalize_a(a_start)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = exact_score_a(a)
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    total = max(60.0, float(deadline) - t0 - 12.0)
    n = len(a)
    print(f"[{label}] start score={best_sc:.8f} n={n}")

    stages = [
        (120.0, 0.04),
        (400.0, 0.07),
        (1500.0, 0.09),
        (6000.0, 0.11),
        (20000.0, 0.13),
        (70000.0, 0.15),
        (250000.0, 0.16),
        (800000.0, 0.25),
    ]

    cum = 0.0
    for beta, frac in stages:
        cumulative = min(1.0, cum + frac)
        cum = cumulative
        sub_dead = t0 + cumulative * total
        sub_dead = min(float(sub_dead), float(deadline) - 6.0)

        while time.monotonic() < sub_dead - 4.0:
            rem = max(0.0, sub_dead - time.monotonic())
            mf = estimate_maxfun(rem, n)
            if mf < 50:
                break
            sc, a2 = run_lbfgs(a, beta, sub_dead, mf)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = float(sc)
                best_a = a2.copy()
                print(
                    f"[{label}] new best {best_sc:.8f} beta={beta:.0f} "
                    f"elapsed={time.monotonic() - t0:.0f}s"
                )
            a = normalize_a(a2)

        if time.monotonic() < sub_dead - 8.0:
            sc_sg, a_sg = subgrad_polish_soft(a, max_iters=18, deadline=sub_dead)
            if np.isfinite(sc_sg) and sc_sg < best_sc:
                best_sc = float(sc_sg)
                best_a = a_sg.copy()
                print(f"[{label}] soft-subgrad best {best_sc:.8f} after beta={beta:.0f}")
            a = normalize_a(a_sg)

    return float(best_sc), best_a


def meta_affine(x, n, r):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, int(n))
    val = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if x.size >= 1:
        val += 0.25 * float(x[0])

    idx = 1
    for f in range(1, int(r) + 1):
        if idx + 1 >= x.size:
            break
        amp = float(x[idx])
        ph = float(x[idx + 1])
        val += amp * np.cos(2.0 * math.pi * f * t + ph)
        idx += 2

    val = np.nan_to_num(val, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=0.0)
    val = np.clip(val, 0.0, None)
    if float(val.sum()) < 1e-10:
        val = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    return normalize_a(val)


def meta_log(x, n, r):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, int(n))
    logv = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    idx = 0
    for f in range(1, int(r) + 1):
        if idx + 1 >= x.size:
            break
        amp = float(x[idx])
        ph = float(x[idx + 1])
        logv += amp * np.cos(2.0 * math.pi * f * t + ph)
        idx += 2

    logv = np.nan_to_num(logv, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    logv = np.clip(logv, -35.0, 35.0)
    a = np.exp(logv - float(np.max(logv)))
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    if float(a.sum()) < 1e-12:
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    return normalize_a(a)


def meta_gaussians(x, n, r):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, int(n))
    val = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if x.size >= 1:
        val = 1.0 + 0.25 * float(x[0])

    idx = 1
    for _ in range(int(r)):
        if idx + 2 >= x.size:
            break
        center = 0.5 + 0.5 * float(np.tanh(x[idx]))
        width = 0.02 + 0.30 * float(np.exp(0.6 * x[idx + 1]))
        amp = float(x[idx + 2])
        val += amp * np.exp(-(((t - center) / width) ** 2))
        idx += 3

    val = np.nan_to_num(val, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=0.0)
    val = np.clip(val, 0.0, None)
    if float(val.sum()) < 1e-10:
        val = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    return normalize_a(val)


def meta_objective(x):
    if META_N <= 0:
        return 1e12
    try:
        if META_MODE == 1:
            a = meta_affine(x, META_N, META_R)
        elif META_MODE == 2:
            a = meta_log(x, META_N, META_R)
        else:
            a = meta_gaussians(x, META_N, META_R)
    except Exception:
        return 1e12
    sc = exact_score_a(a)
    if not np.isfinite(sc) or sc > 1e8:
        return 1e12
    return sc


def run_meta_de(n, mode, r, budget, seed):
    if n <= 0 or budget < 30.0:
        return None, float("inf")

    global META_N, META_R, META_MODE
    META_N = int(n)
    META_R = int(r)
    META_MODE = int(mode)

    try:
        if mode == 1:
            bounds = [(-2.5, 2.5)]
            for _ in range(r):
                bounds.append((-4.0, 4.0))
                bounds.append((-math.pi, math.pi))
        elif mode == 2:
            bounds = []
            for _ in range(r):
                bounds.append((-9.0, 9.0))
                bounds.append((-math.pi, math.pi))
        else:
            bounds = [(-3.0, 3.0)]
            for _ in range(r):
                bounds.append((-6.0, 6.0))
                bounds.append((-3.5, 1.0))
                bounds.append((-4.0, 4.0))
    except Exception:
        return None, float("inf")

    est = estimate_meta_time(n)
    maxfev = int(max(2000, min(14000, (0.75 * float(max(1.0, budget))) / max(est, 1e-4))))

    try:
        res = differential_evolution(
            meta_objective,
            bounds,
            maxfev=maxfev,
            popsize=8,
            tol=1e-6,
            seed=int(seed),
            polish=False,
        )
    except Exception:
        return None, float("inf")

    if getattr(res, "x", None) is not None:
        if mode == 1:
            a = meta_affine(res.x, META_N, META_R)
        elif mode == 2:
            a = meta_log(res.x, META_N, META_R)
        else:
            a = meta_gaussians(res.x, META_N, META_R)
        a = normalize_a(a)
        sc = exact_score_a(a)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            return a, float(sc)
    return None, float("inf")


def update_best(best_seq, best_score, seq, sc):
    if seq is None:
        return best_seq, best_score
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if arr.size == 0:
            return best_seq, best_score
        sc = float(sc)
    except Exception:
        return best_seq, best_score
    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
        return normalize_a(arr).copy(), sc
    return best_seq, best_score


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    total_budget = float(max(60.0, budget_s))
    deadline = t_start + total_budget - 25.0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))

    parent = None
    try:
        h = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
        if h is not None:
            p = np.asarray(h, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            p = np.nan_to_num(p, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=0.0)
            p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
            if p.size >= 64 and float(p.sum()) >= 0.01:
                parent = normalize_a(p)
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    if parent is not None:
        target_len = int(parent.size)
        best_seq = parent.copy()
        best_score = exact_score_a(best_seq)
        print(f"Parent available, length={target_len}, score={best_score:.8f}")
    else:
        target_len = 10240
        best_seq = np.ones(target_len, dtype=np.float64) / float(target_len)
        best_score = exact_score_a(best_seq)
        print("No parent; using constant length 10240, score=2.00000000")

    try:
        calibrate_exact_time(best_seq)
    except Exception:
        calibrate_exact_time(np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64))

    # 1) Strong quick polish from the parent.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 260.0:
        sub_dead = time.monotonic() + min(220.0, max(30.0, (deadline - time.monotonic() - 260.0) * 0.35))
        sc, seq = quick_optimize(best_seq, sub_dead)
        best_seq, best_score = update_best(best_seq, best_score, seq, sc)
        print(f"After initial quick polish: {best_score:.8f}")

    # 2) Low-dimensional global meta-shapes, then full LSE polish.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 360.0:
        meta_specs = [
            (2, 8, 230, 70),   # log trig
            (1, 6, 190, 60),   # affine trig
            (3, 4, 150, 40),   # Gaussians
        ]
        for mode, r, de_budget, polish_budget in meta_specs:
            now = time.monotonic()
            if now > deadline - 320.0:
                break
            avail_de = min(float(de_budget), max(0.0, (deadline - now - 320.0) * 0.30))
            if avail_de < 35.0:
                continue
            a, sc = run_meta_de(target_len, mode, r, avail_de, int(seed) + 1000 * mode)
            if a is None:
                continue
            best_seq, best_score = update_best(best_seq, best_score, a, sc)
            print(f"Meta mode {mode} r={r} before polish: {sc:.8f}")
            now = time.monotonic()
            avail_pol = min(float(polish_budget), max(0.0, (deadline - now - 260.0) * 0.25))
            if avail_pol > 25.0:
                sc2, seq2 = quick_optimize(a, now + avail_pol)
                best_seq, best_score = update_best(best_seq, best_score, seq2, sc2)
                print(f"Meta mode {mode} after polish: {best_score:.8f}")

    # 3) Main long-duration trajectory continuation from the best sequence.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 360.0:
        main_budget = min(
            1050.0,
            max(120.0, (deadline - time.monotonic() - 360.0) * 0.72),
        )
        sc, seq = optimize_trajectory(best_seq, time.monotonic() + main_budget, rng, "main")
        best_seq, best_score = update_best(best_seq, best_score, seq, sc)
        print(f"After main trajectory: {best_score:.8f}")

    # 4) Soft active-set multiplicative polish.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 120.0:
        sg_dead = time.monotonic() + min(280.0, max(30.0, (deadline - time.monotonic() - 120.0) * 0.45))
        sc, seq = subgrad_polish_soft(best_seq, max_iters=120, deadline=sg_dead)
        best_seq, best_score = update_best(best_seq, best_score, seq, sc)
        print(f"After soft subgradient polish: {best_score:.8f}")

    # 5) A few perturbative restarts.
    restarts_done = 0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 110.0 and restarts_done < 4:
        left = max(0.0, deadline - 110.0 - time.monotonic())
        chunk = left / float(4 - restarts_done)
        if chunk < 45.0:
            break
        p = perturb_best(best_seq, rng)
        sc, seq = quick_optimize(p, time.monotonic() + chunk)
        best_seq, best_score = update_best(best_seq, best_score, seq, sc)
        print(f"Perturb restart {restarts_done + 1}: {best_score:.8f}")
        restarts_done += 1

    # 6) Final very-high-beta LSE cleanup.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 35.0:
        sc, seq = run_lbfgs(best_seq, 750000.0, deadline)
        best_seq, best_score = update_best(best_seq, best_score, seq, sc)
        print(f"After final high-beta LSE: {best_score:.8f}")

    # Final cleanup.
    out_arr = np.asarray(best_seq, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    out_arr = np.nan_to_num(out_arr, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    out_arr = np.clip(out_arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(out_arr.sum())
    if s < 0.01:
        out_arr = np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64) * 0.5
    else:
        out_arr = out_arr / s
        out_arr = np.clip(out_arr, 0.0, 1000.0)

    out = [float(v) for v in out_arr]

    try:
        verified = float(evaluate_sequence(out))
    except Exception:
        verified = float("inf")

    print(
        f"FINAL: internal={best_score:.8f} verified={verified:.8f} "
        f"length={len(out)} time={time.monotonic() - t_start:.1f}s"
    )
    return out
```
